"""Canonical "battery pocket" scaffold for the DeWalt battery holder (solid 0).

Reproduces the ORIGINAL solid's global coordinate frame exactly:
bbox X[-121,-59], Y[25,44], Z[0,88.084]  -- do not re-center.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import cadquery as cq

from booleans import fast_cut


@dataclass
class PocketParams:
    # --- outer envelope anchor & extent (matches original global frame) ---
    x_min: float = -121.0        # left outer face X
    width: float = 62.0          # X extent (right outer face = x_min+width = -59)
    y_front: float = 25.0        # front face Y
    depth: float = 19.0          # Y extent, front->back (back face = y_front+depth = 44)
    z_top: float = 88.084        # overall height (top of back wall / roof back edge)

    # --- base slab (Z band with NO cavities at all, full footprint solid) ---
    base_full_h: float = 6.5     # Z 0..6.5 fully solid slab
    apron_h: float = 4.5         # Z 6.5..11 front apron (slots open only for Y>=slot_open_y)
    notch_top: float = 14.0      # Z 11..14 apron band w/ 2 small mouth notches; = cavity_floor
    slot_open_y: float = 30.5    # Y beyond which the two end-slots are open (below cavity_floor)

    # --- outer end walls / envelope profile (S-curve blend front-wall -> roof) ---
    wall_th: float = 4.3         # thickness of each outer end wall (X), also outer shell thickness
    front_wall_h: float = 67.31  # Z height of vertical front wall (at Y=y_front) before blend
    fillet_r1: float = 14.5      # lower S-curve blend radius (front wall -> mid)
    fillet_r2: float = 13.0      # upper S-curve blend radius (mid -> roof)
    roof_y_lo: float = 30.46     # Y where flat roof plane begins (front/low edge)
    roof_z_lo: float = 78.31     # Z of roof plane at roof_y_lo (low point of roof)
    # roof plane runs from (roof_y_lo, roof_z_lo) to (y_front+depth, z_top) -- back top corner is sharp

    # --- through-slots inboard of each end wall ---
    slot_w: float = 9.5          # slot width (X)
    slot_z_hi: float = 64.5      # slot cavity top (above this, structure closes solid)
    notch_w: float = 2.0         # small corner notch width (X) at slot mouths, Z 11..14
    notch_y_depth: float = 3.5   # notch depth from front face (Y direction)

    # --- middle rib comb (between the two slots) ---
    rib_block_x_lo: float = -104.0   # X extent of rib/back-block (absolute, independent of x_min)
    rib_block_x_hi: float = -76.0
    rib_count: int = 4
    rib_width: float = 4.0
    rib_gap: float = 4.0
    rib_tip_y: float = 34.5      # front-most Y of individual rib teeth (open-gap region starts)
    rib_merge_y: float = 36.0    # Y beyond which ribs merge into one solid back block
    rib_z_lo: float = 14.0       # rib comb bottom (= notch_top / cavity_floor)
    upper_merge_z_hi: float = 69.0  # Z up to which slots/ribs/end-walls fuse into one solid band

    # --- thin walls connecting rib block to each slot ---
    side_wall_w: float = 3.2

    # --- back wall (thin skin above the rib comb, below/around the roof) ---
    back_wall_inner_y: float = 40.0   # inner face of the thin back wall (back face is y_front+depth)

    # --- two identical through-holes (main bore + 45deg countersink + small back bore) ---
    hole_x: float = -90.0             # centerline X (envelope mid, -x_min - width/2)
    hole_big_d: float = 10.0
    hole_small_d: float = 5.2
    hole_z_lo: float = 26.656
    hole_z_hi: float = 51.656
    hole_chamfer_deg: float = 45.0
    hole_small_depth: float = 3.19    # Y depth of the small-diameter back counterbore (from back face)

    # --- raised pedestal block under the roof (self-supporting overhang cap) ---
    block_x_lo: float = -102.3
    block_x_hi: float = -77.7
    block_z_lo: float = 66.42
    block_corner_r: float = 7.0       # corner blend radius on the block's top corners (measured, not applied)


def _arc_midpoint(center, radius, p_start, p_end):
    """Exact point on the circle (center, radius) angularly halfway from p_start to
    p_end, taking the SHORT way around -- used to feed cadquery's threePointArc,
    which (unlike radiusArc) has no direction/sign ambiguity."""
    import math
    a0 = math.atan2(p_start[1] - center[1], p_start[0] - center[0])
    a1 = math.atan2(p_end[1] - center[1], p_end[0] - center[0])
    da = a1 - a0
    while da > math.pi:
        da -= 2 * math.pi
    while da < -math.pi:
        da += 2 * math.pi
    am = a0 + da / 2
    return (center[0] + radius * math.cos(am), center[1] + radius * math.sin(am))


def _profile_wire(p: PocketParams) -> cq.Wire:
    """2D outer-envelope profile in the Y-Z plane (front wall -> S-curve -> roof -> back -> bottom).

    The S-curve (front wall -> roof) is two TANGENT arcs (measured circle centers,
    NOT a guessed radiusArc sign): r=14.5 centered (Y,Z)=(39.5,67.309), then r=13
    centered (38.071,67.766) -- both back-calculated from the original STEP's
    cylindrical face `Location()`s. threePointArc (start, exact-midpoint, end)
    is unambiguous, unlike radiusArc(end, +/-r) which silently picked the WRONG
    one of the two possible arcs in an earlier version of this file (visibly
    kinked profile, ~800mm3 overshoot at the roof -- see pitfalls.md).
    """
    y0 = p.y_front
    y1 = p.y_front + p.depth
    pt_wall_top = (y0, p.front_wall_h)
    c1 = (39.5, 67.309)
    c2 = (38.071, 67.766)
    pt_mid = (25.691, 71.731)         # shared tangent point between the two arcs
    pt_roof_lo = (p.roof_y_lo, p.roof_z_lo)
    pt_back_top = (y1, p.z_top)

    m1 = _arc_midpoint(c1, p.fillet_r1, pt_wall_top, pt_mid)
    m2 = _arc_midpoint(c2, p.fillet_r2, pt_mid, pt_roof_lo)

    res = (
        cq.Workplane("YZ")
        .moveTo(y0, 0)
        .lineTo(*pt_wall_top)
        .threePointArc(m1, pt_mid)
        .threePointArc(m2, pt_roof_lo)
        .lineTo(*pt_back_top)
        .lineTo(y1, 0)
        .close()
    )
    return res



def _safe_union(a: cq.Workplane, b: cq.Workplane) -> cq.Workplane:
    """a.union(b), skipping b if an extreme perturbation drove its
    intersect(envelope) to empty (cadquery's .union() raises on an empty
    operand instead of treating it as a no-op)."""
    if not b.val().Solids():
        return a
    return a.union(b)

def build_pocket(p: PocketParams = PocketParams()) -> cq.Workplane:
    y1 = p.y_front + p.depth  # back face Y
    ztop_eps = p.z_top + 5.0

    # 1) Full outer envelope (constant Y-Z cross section extruded across the full X width).
    envelope = _profile_wire(p).extrude(p.width).translate((p.x_min, 0, 0))

    # 2) Base slab band (Z 0..notch_top) is already fully solid inside envelope (front wall
    #    reaches down to Z=0), so nothing to add there.

    # 3) Carve the front-middle cavity, inboard of the two end walls, above notch_top, from
    #    the front up to the back-wall inner face -- this removes the rib gaps and the "fake"
    #    S-curve/roof material that only really exists near the two end walls.
    inner_x_lo = p.x_min + p.wall_th
    inner_x_hi = p.x_min + p.width - p.wall_th
    y1 = p.y_front + p.depth
    center_x_lo = p.rib_block_x_lo - p.side_wall_w
    center_x_hi = p.rib_block_x_hi + p.side_wall_w
    cavity_cut = (
        cq.Workplane("XY")
        .box(center_x_hi - center_x_lo, max(p.back_wall_inner_y - (p.y_front - 1.0), 0.1), max(ztop_eps - p.notch_top, 0.1), centered=False)
        .translate((center_x_lo, p.y_front - 1.0, p.notch_top))
    )
    solid = envelope.cut(cavity_cut)

    # 3c) The "gap" columns outboard of the rib block (between each end wall and the rib
    #     block/side-wall) are ALSO hollow above the merge band -- point-classification
    #     probing of the original showed these are NOT solid envelope material up there
    #     (only a thin sliver survives near the very back face, which back_wall_top below
    #     does not reach because its X range is center-only). Rather than hand-derive that
    #     sliver's exact shape, undershoot here (safe -- residual backfills it) instead of
    #     leaving the full envelope solid (which overshot by ~790mm3 across this exact
    #     region -- see pitfalls.md).
    upper_gap_cut = (
        cq.Workplane("XY")
        .box(inner_x_hi - inner_x_lo, (y1 + 1.0) - (p.y_front - 1.0), ztop_eps - p.upper_merge_z_hi, centered=False)
        .translate((inner_x_lo, p.y_front - 1.0, p.upper_merge_z_hi))
    )
    solid = solid.cut(upper_gap_cut)

    # 3b) The two end slots are genuine through-holes (front to back), from the slot floor
    #    (Z=6.5, below notch_top) all the way up to slot_z_hi -- cut the full slot depth.
    slot_x_positions = [
        (p.x_min + p.wall_th, p.x_min + p.wall_th + p.slot_w),   # left slot
        (p.x_min + p.width - p.wall_th - p.slot_w, p.x_min + p.width - p.wall_th),  # right slot
    ]
    slot_z_cut_hi = p.roof_z_lo  # full-depth slot cavity stays open right up to where the roof/S-curve begins
    for lo, hi in slot_x_positions:
        slot_cut = (
            cq.Workplane("XY")
            .box(hi - lo, (y1 + 1.0) - (p.y_front - 1.0), slot_z_cut_hi - p.base_full_h, centered=False)
            .translate((lo, p.y_front - 1.0, p.base_full_h))
        )
        # fast_cut, not plain .cut() -- a plain cut here silently left a chunk of the
        # shallow front (Y front..roof_y_lo, Z up to ~64.5) un-subtracted despite this
        # box nominally covering that whole region (same coincident-seam boolean
        # failure as the hole bores -- see pitfalls.md). Re-wrap as a plain single-
        # solid Workplane afterward so later .cut()/.union() chaining still works
        # (fast_cut's Compound-wrapped result confuses cadquery's findSolid()).
        solid = fast_cut(solid, slot_cut)
        solid = cq.Workplane(obj=solid.val().Solids()[0])

    # 4) Small mouth notches at the two slot fronts (Z 11..notch_top).
    for lo, hi in slot_x_positions:
        cx = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        for nx in (cx - p.slot_w / 2 + p.notch_w / 2, cx + p.slot_w / 2 - p.notch_w / 2):
            notch = (
                cq.Workplane("XY")
                .box(p.notch_w, p.notch_y_depth, p.notch_top - (p.notch_top - 3.0), centered=False)
                .translate((nx - p.notch_w / 2, p.y_front, p.notch_top - 3.0))
            )
            solid = solid.cut(notch)

    # 5) (Slots above slot_z_hi are already left solid by construction -- the center cavity
    #    cut above only spans the rib-block+side-wall X range, and the slot cut above only
    #    removes material up to slot_z_hi, so nothing further is needed here.)

    # 6) Rib comb: 4 teeth (front sliver, gaps between) + solid backing block behind them.
    rib_pitch = p.rib_width + p.rib_gap
    rib_x0 = p.rib_block_x_lo
    for i in range(p.rib_count):
        xlo = rib_x0 + i * rib_pitch
        tooth = (
            cq.Workplane("XY")
            .box(p.rib_width, y1 - p.rib_tip_y, p.upper_merge_z_hi - p.rib_z_lo, centered=False)

def _vertex_key(v: np.ndarray) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    return (round(float(v[0]), 6), round(float(v[1]), 6), round(float(v[2]), 6))


def _topology_metrics(
    triangles: np.ndarray,
    areas: np.ndarray,
    assumptions: PrintabilityAssumptions,
) -> dict:
    edge_to_triangles: dict[tuple[tuple[float, float, float], tuple[float, float, float]], list[int]] = {}
    for i, tri in enumerate(triangles):
        keys = [_vertex_key(v) for v in tri]
        for j in range(3):
            e = tuple(sorted((keys[j], keys[(j + 1) % 3])))
            edge_to_triangles.setdefault(e, []).append(i)

    bad_edges = 0
    bad_edge_length = 0.0
    for edge, refs in edge_to_triangles.items():
        if len(refs) != 2:
            bad_edges += 1
            a = np.asarray(edge[0], dtype=float)
            b = np.asarray(edge[1], dtype=float)
            bad_edge_length += float(np.linalg.norm(b - a))
    watertight = bad_edges == 0

    parent = list(range(len(triangles)))

    def find(x: int) -> int:
        while parent[x] != x:
            parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]
            x = parent[x]
        return x

    def union(a: int, b: int) -> None:
        ra, rb = find(a), find(b)
        if ra != rb:
            parent[rb] = ra

    for refs in edge_to_triangles.values():
        if len(refs) >= 2:
            base = refs[0]
            for other in refs[1:]:
                union(base, other)

    comps: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
    for i in range(len(triangles)):
        comps.setdefault(find(i), []).append(i)
    component_areas = sorted((float(np.sum(areas[idxs])) for idxs in comps.values()), reverse=True)
    total_area = float(np.sum(areas))
    tiny_threshold = total_area * assumptions.tiny_component_area_fraction
    tiny_components = sum(1 for area in component_areas[1:] if area <= tiny_threshold)
    return {
        "watertight": watertight,
        "bad_edge_count": bad_edges,
        "bad_edge_length_mm": bad_edge_length,
        "connected_components": len(component_areas),
        "component_areas": component_areas,
        "tiny_components": tiny_components,
    }


def _score_mesh(
    path: Path,
    triangles: np.ndarray,
    assumptions: PrintabilityAssumptions,
) -> dict:
    hard_failures: list[str] = []
    risk_factors: list[str] = []
    score = 10.0
    cap = 10.0

    if triangles.size == 0:
        hard_failures.append("mesh has no triangles")
        return _mesh_report(path, 0.0, hard_failures, risk_factors, {"triangle_count": 0}, assumptions)
    if not np.isfinite(triangles).all():
        hard_failures.append("mesh contains non-finite coordinates")
        return _mesh_report(path, 0.0, hard_failures, risk_factors, {"triangle_count": int(len(triangles))}, assumptions)

    tri = _triangle_metrics(triangles)
    topo = _topology_metrics(triangles, tri["areas"], assumptions)
    mins = np.min(triangles.reshape(-1, 3), axis=0)
    maxs = np.max(triangles.reshape(-1, 3), axis=0)
    extents = maxs - mins
    triangle_count = int(len(triangles))

    metrics = {
        "triangle_count": triangle_count,
        "bbox_min": [round(float(x), 4) for x in mins],
        "bbox_max": [round(float(x), 4) for x in maxs],
        "bbox_size": [round(float(x), 4) for x in extents],
        "surface_area_mm2": round(tri["surface_area"], 4),
        "volume_mm3": round(tri["volume"], 4),
        "degenerate_triangles": tri["degenerate_triangles"],
        **{k: v for k, v in topo.items() if k != "component_areas"},
    }

    if np.any(extents <= 1e-9):
        hard_failures.append("mesh has zero thickness in at least one axis")
        cap = min(cap, 3.0)
    if not topo["watertight"]:
        bad_length = float(topo["bad_edge_length_mm"])
        bad_ratio = bad_length / math.sqrt(tri["surface_area"]) if tri["surface_area"] > 0 else 0.0
        metrics["bad_edge_length_mm"] = round(bad_length, 4)
        metrics["bad_boundary_surface_ratio"] = round(bad_ratio, 6)
        if (
            bad_ratio >= assumptions.severe_bad_boundary_surface_ratio
            or int(topo["bad_edge_count"]) >= assumptions.severe_bad_edge_count
        ):
            hard_failures.append("mesh has severe open/non-manifold boundaries")
            cap = min(cap, 4.0)
        else:
            risk_factors.append("mesh has minor non-watertight edges")
    if tri["volume"] <= 1e-9:
        hard_failures.append("mesh has zero enclosed volume")
        cap = min(cap, 3.0)
    if tri["degenerate_triangles"]:
        risk_factors.append(f"{tri['degenerate_triangles']} degenerate triangles")
        score -= min(1.5, 0.05 * tri["degenerate_triangles"])
    if topo["connected_components"] > 1:
        risk_factors.append(f"{topo['connected_components']} disconnected mesh components")
        score -= min(2.5, 0.4 * (topo["connected_components"] - 1))
    if topo["tiny_components"]:
        risk_factors.append(f"{topo['tiny_components']} tiny disconnected components")
        score -= min(2.0, 0.5 * topo["tiny_components"])

    orientation = _orientation_metrics(
        triangles,
        tri["areas"],
        tri["normals"],
        tri["surface_area"],
        assumptions,
    )
    bed_area = orientation["bed_area"]
    metrics["selected_orientation"] = orientation["name"]
    metrics["bed_contact_area_mm2"] = round(bed_area, 4)
    if bed_area < assumptions.min_bed_contact_area_mm2:
        risk_factors.append(
            f"low bed contact area ({bed_area:.2f} mm^2 < {assumptions.min_bed_contact_area_mm2:.2f} mm^2)"
        )

def gen_step():
    p = Params()
    validate_params(p)

    parts = [
        build_frame(p),
        build_trigger(p),
        build_nock_bar(p),
        build_rails(p),
        build_ladder(p),
        build_bow_limb(p),
        build_stock_plates(p),
        build_pin_sockets(p),
        build_tabs(p),
    ]
    result = parts[0]
    for part in parts[1:]:
        result = fast_union(result, part)
    return result


if __name__ == "__main__":
    gen_step()

from pathlib import Path
import sys, json, numpy as np
sys.path.insert(0, 'skills/evaluate-cad-reconstruction')
from cad_reconstruction_eval.printability_score import _read_stl, _triangle_metrics, _topology_metrics, PrintabilityAssumptions, _vertex_key

def comps(triangles):
    edge_to_triangles={}
    for i, tri in enumerate(triangles):
        keys=[_vertex_key(v) for v in tri]
        for j in range(3):
            e=tuple(sorted((keys[j], keys[(j+1)%3])))
            edge_to_triangles.setdefault(e,[]).append(i)
    parent=list(range(len(triangles)))
    def find(x):
        while parent[x]"'!=x:
            parent[x]=parent[parent[x]]; x=parent[x]
        return x
    def union(a,b):
        ra,rb=find(a),find(b)
        if ra!=rb: parent[rb]=ra
    for refs in edge_to_triangles.values():
        if len(refs)>=2:
            base=refs[0]
            for other in refs[1:]: union(base,other)
    out={}
    for i in range(len(triangles)):
        out.setdefault(find(i),[]).append(i)
    return [triangles[idxs] for idxs in out.values()]

def write_ascii_stl(path, tris, name):
    with open(path,'"'w') as f:
        f.write(f'solid {name}\\n')
        for tri in tris:
            n=np.cross(tri[1]-tri[0], tri[2]-tri[0])
            l=np.linalg.norm(n)
            if l: n=n/l
            else: n=np.array([0.0,0.0,0.0])
            f.write(f'  facet normal {n[0]:.9g} {n[1]:.9g} {n[2]:.9g}\\n    outer loop\\n')
            for v in tri:
                f.write(f'      vertex {v[0]:.9g} {v[1]:.9g} {v[2]:.9g}\\n')
            f.write('    endloop\\n  endfacet\\n')
        f.write(f'endsolid {name}\\n')

exec
/bin/zsh -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys, numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageOps
sys.path.insert(0,'skills/evaluate-cad-reconstruction')
from cad_reconstruction_eval.printability_score import _read_stl
views={
 'top':((0,1),(2,)), 'front':((0,2),(1,)), 'right':((1,2),(0,)),
 'iso':(None,None)
}
def project(tri, view):
    pts=tri.reshape(-1,3)
    if view=='iso':
        # rotate around z then x
        rz=np.deg2rad(35); rx=np.deg2rad(55)
        Rz=np.array([[np.cos(rz),-np.sin(rz),0],[np.sin(rz),np.cos(rz),0],[0,0,1]])
        Rx=np.array([[1,0,0],[0,np.cos(rx),-np.sin(rx)],[0,np.sin(rx),np.cos(rx)]])
        p=pts@Rz.T@Rx.T
        xy=p[:,[0,2]]; depth=p[:,1]
    else:
        axes={'top':(0,1,2),'front':(0,2,1),'right':(1,2,0)}[view]
        xy=pts[:,[axes[0],axes[1]]]; depth=pts[:,axes[2]]
    return xy.reshape(-1,3,2), depth.reshape(-1,3).mean(axis=1)
def render(stl,out,view):
    tri=_read_stl(stl)
    polys,depth=project(tri,view)
    mn=polys.reshape(-1,2).min(axis=0); mx=polys.reshape(-1,2).max(axis=0)
    size=900; margin=40
    scale=min((size-2*margin)/(mx[0]-mn[0] or 1),(size-2*margin)/(mx[1]-mn[1] or 1))
    img=Image.new('RGB',(size,size),'white'); draw=ImageDraw.Draw(img,'RGBA')
    order=np.argsort(depth)
    for idx in order:
        poly=polys[idx]
        p=(poly-mn)*scale+margin
        p[:,1]=size-p[:,1]
        shade=int(150+80*((depth[idx]-depth.min())/(depth.max()-depth.min()+1e-9)))
        draw.polygon([tuple(x) for x in p], fill=(shade,shade,shade,180), outline=(80,80,80,80))
    img.save(out)
for p in ['cricut-material-holder','crossbow-kit-card-fully-printed','customizable-stackable-beer-crate','dewalt-battery-holder']:
    root=Path('outputs/reconstructions/step-to-cadquery')/p
    stl=next(root.glob('*.stl'))
    rdir=root/'.eval_tmp/stl_renders'; rdir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    imgs=[]
    for v in ['iso','top','front','right']:
        out=rdir/f'{v}.png'; render(stl,out,v); imgs.append(Image.open(out).resize((360,360)))
    montage=Image.new('RGB',(720,720),'white')
    for i,img in enumerate(imgs): montage.paste(img,((i%2)*360,(i//2)*360))
    montage.save(rdir/'montage.png')
    print(rdir/'montage.png')
PY" in /Users/macbookpro/Documents/ai-chain/_hardware/cad-skill-test-2
 succeeded in 6922ms:
outputs/reconstructions/step-to-cadquery/cricut-material-holder/.eval_tmp/stl_renders/montage.png
outputs/reconstructions/step-to-cadquery/crossbow-kit-card-fully-printed/.eval_tmp/stl_renders/montage.png
outputs/reconstructions/step-to-cadquery/customizable-stackable-beer-crate/.eval_tmp/stl_renders/montage.png
outputs/reconstructions/step-to-cadquery/dewalt-battery-holder/.eval_tmp/stl_renders/montage.png

So before/after scores are unchanged; no scorer behavior changed and no regression rerun was needed.
